openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r205/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:51:Only bottom-torus tubes are removed to form E. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r205/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:93:Every other component branch attaches to this principal subtree at exactly one vertex. A deck transformation stabilizing that branch fixes its attachment vertex, and the deck action on the original universal tree is free; the stabilizer is therefore trivial. There are finitely many branch types in the compact quotient. Within a branch, two points identified by a deck transformation would give a branch stabilizer, so it embeds in that finite quotient away from its root. This gives the needed finite and uniformly bounded extraneous branches, rather than merely an unproved assertion that finite quotient implies bounded length. Removing their closed cuts and merging pieces leaves connected Y_e with exactly the prescribed torus boundary and keeps every law disk in its vertex piece. This is the operative source argument, with no use of the special cycle's degree or of unit heights.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r205/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:62:For every other terminal intersection in a rim sphere, fix the outer sign epsilon, all earlier eta indices, and the opposing sheet. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/new-twenty-2026-10-31/splitham-review-r1/REVIEW.md:43:There is also a useful limitation. For ANY balanced split graph with side size m, r-connectivity and K₁,r+1-freeness imply all cross-degrees on both sides equal r: independent degrees are ≥ r, clique independent-neighbour counts are ≤ r, and edge totals force equality. Excluding K₁,r+1 + e then forces pairwise neighbourhood intersection ≥ 2. Counting over clique pairs gives m·C(r,2) ≥ 2·C(m,2), hence m ≤ r(r − 1)/2 + 1. Thus there is no balanced witness at r = 4 with order ≥ 15, and none at r = 5 with order > 22. These statements do not rule out unbalanced counterexamples, or other obstructions. The cyclic m = 16 failure at r = 6 does not exclude noncyclic designs.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r199-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:47:is an order and incompatibility isomorphism onto the dense subposet consisting of the empty binary sequence and sequences ending in zero. “Dense subtree” in the submission is used in this subposet sense; downward closure under every binary initial segment is unnecessary. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r199-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:40:**4. For pure k, C_k intersect gamma must be cofinal; for mixed *, for every beta<gamma and integer l there must be an alpha in (beta,gamma) of color greater than l. Countability follows from finite rational data on a countable ordinal, including the metadata.
openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:51:Only bottom-torus tubes are removed to form E. All upper surfaces and all terminal caps remain available. A chosen leaf at depth h in one root branch contracts that branch to a disk with 2^h signed parallel copies of that core. At each node one keeps the child leading to the chosen leaf and uses two opposite parallel copies to fill the cut. The other capped subtree has been omitted from this particular contracted disk, not removed from E. Contracting it therefore supplies a null-homotopy of its attaching loop in E. Intersections of such a null-homotopy with other sheets do not invalidate a statement about π1(E).
openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:91:The connected-cut construction uses the universal tree of the entire finite multigraph Γ_(q,d), not the integer height line. Forgetting the nine extra vertex loops gives its tree cover even when there are higher-offset and parallel edges. The prescribed outside faces, torus collars and disk neighborhoods map to the required tree pieces; the contractible-fiber extension used in N `for:cuts` retains this prescription. The component graph of the preliminary cuts is a tree: its possible graph fundamental group is killed because the cover's remaining group generators are carried within vertex pieces. The original plumbing tree embeds as its principal subtree via the prescribed collars.
openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:93:Every other component branch attaches to this principal subtree at exactly one vertex. A deck transformation stabilizing that branch fixes its attachment vertex, and the deck action on the original universal tree is free; the stabilizer is therefore trivial. There are finitely many branch types in the compact quotient. Within a branch, two points identified by a deck transformation would give a branch stabilizer, so it embeds in that finite quotient away from its root. This gives the needed finite and uniformly bounded extraneous branches, rather than merely an unproved assertion that finite quotient implies bounded length. Removing their closed cuts and merging pieces leaves connected Y_e with exactly the prescribed torus boundary and keeps every law disk in its vertex piece. This is the operative source argument, with no use of the special cycle's degree or of unit heights.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:48:Maintain the following data at each finite rooted subtree: its attaching product annulus, the marked terminal annuli and terminal disk cuts, connector feet outside those cuts, and chosen paths through the connector tree. Grafting a torus product at one terminal annulus replaces one cut by two cuts and adds one terminal generator. Cutting again leaves a ball. No cycle is added to the connector graph, so there is no additional free generator. The attaching collar is a product interface during this graft. Induction produces a boundary handlebody with precisely r longitudinal terminal annuli and their free basis, for any r-leaf binary tree. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:62:For every other terminal intersection in a rim sphere, fix the outer sign epsilon, all earlier eta indices, and the opposing sheet. Therefore the group labels agree in pi_1(E), while the signs epsilon·eta_1···eta_h are opposite. This proves cancellation for each fixed opposing sheet, including one on another rim sphere. No comparison of epsilon=+ with epsilon=− is needed; such a comparison could contain a nontrivial bottom meridian and would be invalid.
openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r204/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:191:The lifted nerve has finitely many H-orbits but infinitely many vertices. A fixed tree vertex belongs to a uniformly finite set of supports. Convex gluing of their Davis complexes over the Bass–Serre tree is CAT(0), locally finite, proper and cocompact for the semidirect product E. Dimension is at most five. This is not the ordinary chamber development used in §5.1.

A fixed tree vertex belongs to a uniformly finite set of supports. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r202/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:62:For every other terminal intersection in a rim sphere, fix the outer sign epsilon, all earlier eta indices, and the opposing sheet. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r196/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:191:The lifted nerve has finitely many H-orbits but infinitely many vertices. A fixed tree vertex belongs to a uniformly finite set of supports. Special Coxeter groups on those finite induced sets, and on their intersections across a tree edge, have colimit the required Coxeter group: connected supports identify each generator, and every defining commutation occurs somewhere without adding extra relations. Convex gluing of their Davis complexes over the Bass–Serre tree is CAT(0), locally finite, proper and cocompact for the semidirect product E. Dimension is at most five. This is not the ordinary chamber development used in §5.1.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r195/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:93:Every other component branch attaches to this principal subtree at exactly one vertex. This gives the needed finite and uniformly bounded extraneous branches, rather than merely an unproved assertion that finite quotient implies bounded length. This is the operative source argument, with no use of the special cycle's degree or of unit heights.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r195/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:62:For every other terminal intersection in a rim sphere, fix the outer sign epsilon, all earlier eta indices, and the opposing sheet. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r195/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:191:The lifted nerve has finitely many H-orbits but infinitely many vertices. The paid support construction chooses equivariant anchors in the free-group Cayley tree and finite-radius subtrees T_u, with adjacent supports intersecting. A fixed tree vertex belongs to a uniformly finite set of supports. Special Coxeter groups on those finite induced sets, and on their intersections across a tree edge, have colimit the required Coxeter group: connected supports identify each generator, and every defining commutation occurs somewhere without adding extra relations. Convex gluing of their Davis complexes over the Bass–Serre tree is CAT(0), locally finite, proper and cocompact for the semidirect product E. Dimension is at most five. This is not the ordinary chamber development used in §5.1.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r193/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:62:For every other terminal intersection in a rim sphere, fix the outer sign epsilon, all earlier eta indices, and the opposing sheet. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r193/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:191:The lifted nerve has finitely many H-orbits but infinitely many vertices. It would be insufficient simply to call its Davis complex cocompact. The paid support construction chooses equivariant anchors in the free-group Cayley tree and finite-radius subtrees T_u, with adjacent supports intersecting. A fixed tree vertex belongs to a uniformly finite set of supports. Special Coxeter groups on those finite induced sets, and on their intersections across a tree edge, have colimit the required Coxeter group: connected supports identify each generator, and every defining commutation occurs somewhere without adding extra relations. Convex gluing of their Davis complexes over the Bass–Serre tree is CAT(0), locally finite, proper and cocompact for the semidirect product E. Dimension is at most five. This is not the ordinary chamber development used in §5.1.

Therefore the required conditional expectations are legitimate without an L2 assumption.

The other capped subtree has been omitted from this particular contracted disk, not removed from E. Contracting it therefore supplies a null-homotopy of its attaching loop in E. openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r191/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r190/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-proof-r1/REVIEW.md:93:Every other component branch attaches to this principal subtree at exactly one vertex. A deck transformation stabilizing that branch fixes its attachment vertex, and the deck action on the original universal tree is free; the stabilizer is therefore trivial. There are finitely many branch types in the compact quotient. Within a branch, two points identified by a deck transformation would give a branch stabilizer, so it embeds in that finite quotient away from its root. This gives the needed finite and uniformly bounded extraneous branches, rather than merely an unproved assertion that finite quotient implies bounded length. This is the operative source argument, with no use of the special cycle's degree or of unit heights.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r191/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/lanes/sol-fresh-source-discovery-r190/originals/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-14/structural-r196-contribution-r1/REVIEW.md:62:For every other terminal intersection in a rim sphere, fix the outer sign epsilon, all earlier eta indices, and the opposing sheet. A fixed tree vertex belongs to a uniformly finite set of supports. Special Coxeter groups on those finite induced sets, and on their intersections across a tree edge, have colimit the required Coxeter group: connected supports identify each generator, and every defining commutation occurs somewhere without adding extra relations. This is not the ordinary chamber development used in §5.1.

A fixed tree vertex belongs to a uniformly finite set of supports. Special Coxeter groups on those finite induced sets, and on their intersections across a tree edge, have colimit the required Coxeter group: connected supports identify each generator, and every defining commutation occurs somewhere without adding extra relations. This is not the ordinary chamber development used in §5.1.

openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/new-twenty-2026-10-31/pdtar-formal-r2b/results.json:14:  "proof_route": "Complete Hamming-walk isometry replacement; co-singleton coordinate basis; affine reconstruction; upward-family flip normalization; literal-round graph transport. No cube interval classification assumed.",
openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/new-twenty-2026-10-31/pdtar-formal-r2b/results.json:17:    "PDTAR.ReductionHammingRemaining by PDTAR.reductionHammingRemaining"
openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/new-twenty-2026-10-31/pdtar-formal-r2b/SUMMARY.md:19:3. Any two PD sets S,T have a TAR walk of Hamming length: add vertices to reach their union, then reverse a monotone walk from T to the union. The monotone walk is proved by strong induction on the number of missing vertices; PD up-closure guarantees every intermediate vertex. The symmetric-difference triangle inequality bounds every TAR walk below by its endpoint Hamming distance. Map the constructed walks along the isomorphism and its inverse to obtain equality of all endpoint Hamming distances. Thus `ReductionHammingRemaining`, introduced as a specification in ReductionMetric, is fully discharged by `reductionHammingRemaining` in ReductionPaths. It is not a remaining assumption.
openai-proof-research/frontier/campaigns/2026-10-27/new-discoveries-batch2/new-twenty-2026-10-31/pdtar-formal-r2b/SUMMARY.md:20:4. The Hamming distances to the full set and each co-singleton determine membership of each coordinate. Toggling a coordinate decreases Hamming weight exactly when the coordinate is present. Applying this test before and after the TAR isomorphism gives its full affine description: phi(S) = e(S) symmetric-difference R, where R = A symmetric-difference univ. This proves `reduction_affine_of_hamming`. Surjectivity yields the corresponding iff on all finite subsets, not merely the forward implication on PD sets.
